[00:00:00.000 - 00:00:05.000] [microphone identity check redacted]
[00:00:05 - 00:00:10] Previously in The Vengeful.
[00:00:10 - 00:00:15] The struggle for House Rannek has become a war against certainty itself.
[00:00:15 - 00:00:26] Marchioness Maria Rannek reached the Western Admiralty dockyards with Sapphire, Andrew, Oren Bell, Trajan, Sister Pira Tradewise, and Sir Erik Malmann.
[00:00:26 - 00:00:39] The facility ought to have offered soldiers, ships, naval authority, and a defensible place from which to answer the crisis at Castle Rannek.
[00:00:39 - 00:00:51] Instead, it offered an anxious lesson. An institution can retain its uniforms, its codes, its chain of command after someone else has learned to wear them.
[00:00:51 - 00:01:04] Marchioness Maria entered under her own authority, only to be accused of imposture by a supposed marshal and a woman claiming to be Gwyniviere Rannek.
[00:01:04 - 00:01:09] The false Gwyniviere would not withstand the simplest test of her stolen life.
[00:01:09 - 00:01:14] When challenged to paint, she hid behind an injured hand.
[00:01:14 - 00:01:20] When offered healing, she had no answer that made the excuse less suspicious.
[00:01:20 - 00:01:25] Sapphire's Moonbeam exposed her as a shapechanger.
[00:01:25 - 00:01:30] Andrew drove the marshal into the same light and exposed him as another.
[00:01:30 - 00:01:41] Trajan, a Face-Stealer himself, revealed his true form just long enough to unsettle the captives before returning to Magistrate Tonon's stolen appearance.
[00:01:41 - 00:01:52] The supposed Mark Malthrek proved not to be Mark at all, but one of the lifelike flesh golems associated with Crispin Mallow.
[00:01:52 - 00:01:59] Oren's absurd command, “and his name was Rumpelstiltskin,” dropped it where it stood.
[00:01:59 - 00:02:06] The captives had poison-filled false teeth meant to turn interrogation into silence.
[00:02:06 - 00:02:09] Trajan removed them before they could be used.
[00:02:09 - 00:02:12] Their answers did not make the situation gentler.
[00:02:12 - 00:02:19] Reformist agents possessed valid naval authentication despite Maria still holding her signet,
[00:02:19 - 00:02:24] and some unspecified credential had been taken from Marquess Uther or Lance Rannek.
[00:02:24 - 00:02:35] The command structure had been compromised badly enough that an order to deploy forces into Maiden's Field could no longer be trusted,
[00:02:35 - 00:02:38] merely because it arrived through official channels.
[00:02:38 - 00:02:45] Vice Marshal Tyrion Donmar had recognized the fraud and had been imprisoned for it.
[00:02:45 - 00:02:52] Once freed, he made Maria prove herself in turn.
[00:02:52 - 00:03:00] Not out of cruelty, but because a false marchioness had already appeared at Castle Rannek.
[00:03:00 - 00:03:05] Exhausted by the ritual, Maria nevertheless supplied what he needed.
[00:03:05 - 00:03:10] Donmar resumed command with an impossible brief.
[00:03:10 - 00:03:12] Protect the real marchioness.
[00:03:12 - 00:03:21] Restore a force that could be trusted, and help retake a castle already occupied by enemies who knew how to imitate its people.
[00:03:21 - 00:03:27] The party could identify Face-Stealers by anatomy, but that was only a beginning.
[00:03:27 - 00:03:32] A person who passed such a test might still be another kind of shapeshifter.
[00:03:32 - 00:03:39] Or a bribed officer. Or a forged identity. Or an ordinary traitor.
[00:03:39 - 00:03:45] There would be no single clean ritual that made the base safe.
[00:03:45 - 00:03:49] Meanwhile, the larger danger acquired a name and a method.
[00:03:49 - 00:03:55] Crispin Mallow was identified as a Reformist and the Bearer of the Candle at Castle Malthrek.
[00:03:55 - 00:03:57] A maker of puppets.
[00:03:57 - 00:03:59] A surgeon of horrors.
[00:03:59 - 00:04:01] Puppets!
[00:04:01 - 00:04:03] Continue.
[00:04:03 - 00:04:12] His designs had accelerated against the Ranneks after earlier intervention at Castle Malthrek.
[00:04:12 - 00:04:17] Upset plans that had been intended for a later hour.
[00:04:17 - 00:04:19] The party divided.
[00:04:19 - 00:04:25] Maria, Sapphire, Andrew, Trajan, Sister Pira, and Oren held the naval front.
[00:04:25 - 00:04:29] Hellion Blanchimontt, Sir Hilis Brastok, Angel, and Gwyniviere Rannek.
[00:04:29 - 00:04:30] The baby is crying.
[00:04:30 - 00:04:36] Erik Malmann and their allies prepared to enter Castle Rannek itself.
[00:04:36 - 00:04:43] Angel carried them through with Dimension Door, humming the Rannek Anthem into the throne room.
[00:04:43 - 00:04:52] They arrived to an empty throne, startled guards, and the feeling that an alarm had already begun to spread through the castle.
[00:04:52 - 00:04:57] A figure wearing Sandor Kethlas's face came forward and embraced Angel.
[00:04:57 - 00:05:02] Angel and Hellion knew at once that it was not Sandor.
[00:05:02 - 00:05:10] The thing unfolded into a grotesque composite of displaced limbs, stolen flesh, and shifting organs.
[00:05:10 - 00:05:16] It knew enough private history to taunt Brastok and plead with Angel.
[00:05:16 - 00:05:24] It fought with a detached jaw like a blade, scattered itself into crawling pieces, and remade its body when struck.
[00:05:24 - 00:05:29] Yet the party found its undoing in restoration rather than destruction.
[00:05:29 - 00:05:34] Healing magic made the creature reject what had been forced together.
[00:05:34 - 00:05:38] A healer's touch caused a limb to drop away.
[00:05:38 - 00:05:47] Cure Wounds separated much of its lower body, and even a healing draught broke its jaw and the surrounding tissue apart.
[00:05:47 - 00:05:59] When the false Sandor finally fell, the party discovered that his face had been worn over another shapeshifter's dappled skin.
[00:05:59 - 00:06:03] Then the immediate horror gave way to another.
[00:06:03 - 00:06:13] A heart-like fragment within the remains belonged to an urdefolcin, an ancient non-humanoid protector of forest communities.
[00:06:13 - 00:06:17] Hags had used such fragments in rituals and curses.
[00:06:17 - 00:06:21] Oren already knew Mallow had traded with the hags for them.
[00:06:21 - 00:06:27] Now he understood that the fragments had been fed into Mallow's puppets.
[00:06:27 - 00:06:32] The castle's refuge held a more intimate catastrophe.
[00:06:32 - 00:06:42] There, the party found Stacy Cartwright, terrified, altered, and still conscious inside a body that she could not command.
[00:06:42 - 00:06:52] Legend Lore revealed a bargain made in the belief that she was protecting the Malthreks, followed by Mallow's more brutal control.
[00:06:52 - 00:07:04] It also revealed the presence of Auraxx, a gold dragon whose body had been carved from the manifested form of a cursed gold-canary prison and grafted,
[00:07:04 - 00:07:11] in part, into Stacy before Mallow took the greater portion for himself.
[00:07:11 - 00:07:18] Auraxx had crossed the Realm Bridge in defiance of draconic orders to protect fey refugees.
[00:07:18 - 00:07:21] The curse had anticipated his choice.
[00:07:21 - 00:07:24] His soul remains imprisoned in the damaged canary.
[00:07:24 - 00:07:28] His dismantled body cannot simply be restored.
[00:07:28 - 00:07:33] Stacy's body, meanwhile, erupted into counterfeit dragon anatomy.
[00:07:33 - 00:07:39] Scales, claws, mismatched wings, unstable teleportation, fire, and radiant force.
[00:07:39 - 00:07:44] Greater Restoration cleared her mind, but it did not return control.
[00:07:44 - 00:07:51] The party contained her rather than abandoning her to the thing Mallow had made.
[00:07:51 - 00:07:58] Elsewhere, Oren reconnoitered the compromised installation near Maiden's Field.
[00:07:58 - 00:08:06] He found people who'd escaped an unspecified purge, a perimeter shaped by Face-Stealers and Malthrek-like golems,
[00:08:06 - 00:08:12] and a growing suspicion that the enemy may itself be a shapeshifter.
[00:08:12 - 00:08:17] The party slipped past guards under the distraction of Enthrall, but questions ahead…
